!Sample_title = MII oocyte total RNA rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7504964
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 02 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 22 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 02 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = C57BL/6
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: C57BL/6
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: Oocyte
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = MII_total_rep1
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: bigWig, tab-delimited text files indicating TPM values (for RNA-seq)

!Sample_description = e2C_EU_rep2
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: bigWig, tab-delimited text files indicating TPM values (for RNA-seq)
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: LET-seq
!Sample_platform_id = GPL24247

!Sample_title = 4-cell EU+ RNA rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7504977

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = C57BL/6
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: C57BL/6
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: 4-cell
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.

^SAMPLE = GSM7504983
!Sample_title = Blastocyst EU+ RNA rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7504983
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 02 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 22 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 02 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = C57BL/6
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: C57BL/6
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: Blastocyst
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = Blasto_EU_rep1
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

!Sample_status = Public on Apr 02 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 22 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 02 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = C57BL/6
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: C57BL/6
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: Early 2-cell
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = e2C_UnlabeledRNA_rep2
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: bigWig, tab-delimited text files indicating TPM values (for RNA-seq)
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: LET-seq
!Sample_platform_id = GPL24247
!Sample_contact_name = Mizuki,,Sakamoto
!Sample_contact_email = sakamotom@yamanashi.ac.jp
!Sample_contact_institute = University of Yamanashi
!Sample_contact_address = 4-4-37,Takeda
!Sample_contact_city = Kofu
!Sample_contact_zip/postal_code = 400-8510
!Sample_contact_country = Japan
!Sample_instrument_model = Illumina NovaSeq 6000
!Sample_library_selection = other

For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = Aphi2C_UnlabeledRNA_rep2
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.

The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. ^SAMPLE = GSM7505032
!Sample_title = ESC Obox6 RNA-seq rep2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7505032
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 02 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 22 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 02 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = hybrid C57BL/6 / 129
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: hybrid C57BL/6 / 129
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: Embryonic stem cell
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = ES_Obox6_Rep2
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: bigWig, tab-delimited text files indicating TPM values (for RNA-seq)

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
!Sample_description = ES_Obox8_Rep1
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

!Sample_description = L2C_siObox3_2_rep1
!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.

MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.

!Sample_data_processing = For LET-seq and RNA-seq data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Hisat2 after removing adaptor sequences and low quality reads by Trim Galore! with a ‘--clip_R2 3’ option. After the reads were aligned, the reads correspond to rRNA were removed by using Bedtools intersectBed function. The uniquely mapped reads were isolated by using the tag NH:i:1. featureCounts (version 1.6.5) and GENCODE annotation of the mm10 mouse genome (vM25) was used to analyze transcription levels with options ‘-p -O --fraction’. bigWig files were generated by deeptools bamCoverage with options '-bs 25 --normalizeUsing RPKM -ignore chrX chrM chrY --smoothLength 75'. The expression of each annotated gene was quantified by calculating TPM (transcripts per million) using R.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN data, paired-end reads were aligned to the mouse genome (mm10) using Bowtie2 with options  ‘-X 700 --dovetail’ after removing adaptor sequences and low-quality reads by Trim Galore!. bigWig files were generated by bamCoverage from deepTools with parameters ‘-bs 50 --normalizeUsing RPKM --smoothLength 500’. MACS2 was used to call peaks with options ‘-q 0.05 -f BAMPE’.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: bigWig, tab-delimited text files indicating TPM values (for RNA-seq)

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: wild-type
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq with inhibitors, zygotes were cultured in KSOM containing DMSO, 10 μM A-485 or 100 nM TSA from 18 to 24 hphCG, and then cultured in KSOM medium containing the same inhibitor and 1 mM EU from 24 to 27 hphCG. For RNA-seq of mESC samples, plasmids were transfected into mESCs using Lipofectamine 2000.  For RNA-seq of SCNT embryos, MII oocytes were enucleated, and the nuclei of cumulus cells were placed into ooplasm. SCNT embryos were then injected with 10 ng/µl of Obox3 or EGFP mRNA. For CUT&RUN, mESCs were collected at 24 hours after the transfection of pPB-CAG-3xFLAG-Obox3-2A-Puro or pPB-CAG-2A-puro control plasmid.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Early embryos were cultured in KSOM. Male mouse embryonic stem cells were cultured with the medium consists of GMEM containing 15% FBS, 0.1 mM 2-mercaptoethanol, nonessential amino acid, sodium pyruvate, penicillin/streptomycin, LIF, 0.5 uM PD0325901, and 3 uM CHIR99021 on gelatin-coated plates.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For LET-seq and RNA-seq, total RNA from whole-cell lysate was used.  For CUT&RUN, chromatin was digested by protein A- potein G-MNase.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = LET-seq protocol was used. For total RNA-seq, SMART-seq stranded kit (TAKARA) was used. For CUT&RUN library preparation, NEBnext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit (NEB) was used.
